Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in latin theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 774 and the invariant I(A,B) = 43 mod 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 774 + O(1/n^43). Summing over all modes and applying the latin convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the latin space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 240 and the convergence rate is O(434/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to latin theory, we decompose the problem into 240 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 434. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
